R8|  | Wage Type Catalogue
R9|  | Colombia (CO)
~
~
R12|  | Version | Effective Date | Change Description | Status | Updated By
R13|  | 1 | 2026-09-04 | Initial Colombia Wage Type Catalogue for FY2026. All 300 global baseline codes configured fresh for Colombia (CST arts. 127/128 salary classification, the THREE separate bases, the Ley 1393/2010 40% clamp, the ET art. 114-1 exoneration cliff, the ET art. 388 depuration and the art. 383 table) plus 102 Colombia-specific wage types. Social-insurance column expanded into five per-fund columns (Salud / FSP / ARL / Parafiscales / Pension) and three Colombia-specific base columns added (salary flag / statutory-benefit base / vacation base). Includes the Global WTC Coverage sheet and the Form Reference Guide (C17); every tax code defined and sourced to a Colombian authority publication (C9). Wage-type code numbers for Colombia-specific rows are DERIVED (no Mercans code range has been assigned for Colombia). | Released | Mercans Compliance (build_co_wtc.py)
R14|  | 2 | 2025-09-09 | Wage codes Mapping | Released | Devshree Vyas

For a NON-RESIDENT this is withheld at the flat 20% on the gross under ET art. 408 with no depuration (DIAN Concepto 011108 de 2026); the switch to the art. 383 table is prospective from the payment following the 183rd day of presence. | Earning | Salario basico: full month = S; partial month = S x days worked / 30 (30-day commercial month, so February and a 31-day month are both 30 payroll days); minimum 1,750,905/month | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Basico - DiasTrabajados (NIE069), SueldoTrabajado (NIE070) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R68| 62110 | Overtime | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Overtime is SALARY by express statutory definition - CST art. 127 lists 'el valor del trabajo suplementario o de las horas extras' - so it is fully in the IBC and fully in the payroll-levy base, and in the prima / cesantias / interest base. IT IS EXCLUDED FROM THE VACATION BASE: CST art. 192 excludes ONLY 'el valor del trabajo en dias de descanso obligatorio y el valor del trabajo suplementario en horas extras'. Fully taxable - there is no preferential rate and no exempt overtime allowance in Colombia. CST art. 168 num. 4 is critical: each premium 'se produce de manera exclusiva, es decir, sin acumularse con ningun otro', so the night, day-overtime and night-overtime premiums are ALTERNATIVES; only the art. 179 rest-day premium is added to them. Volume limit: 2 hours per day and 12 per week (CST art. 22 of Ley 50 de 1990 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 13), so 56 total weekly hours to 14 July 2026 and 54 from 15 July. Ministry authorisation was abolished by Ley 2466 art. 12 and replaced by a mandatory supplementary-work REGISTER; failure to keep it lets inspectors suspend all overtime for six months. Reported with quantity, start and end time, percentage and amount per hour type. | Earning | Day overtime = (S / D) x 1.25 x H; night overtime = (S / D) x 1.75 x H; on an obligatory rest day add the art. 179 premium (+80% to 30 Jun 2026, +90% from 1 Jul 2026). Volume limit: 2 hours per day and 12 per week (CST art. 22 of Ley 50 de 1990 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 13), so 56 total weekly hours to 14 July 2026 and 54 from 15 July. Ministry authorisation was abolished by Ley 2466 art. 12 and replaced by a mandatory supplementary-work REGISTER; failure to keep it lets inspectors suspend all overtime for six months. Reported with quantity, start and end time, percentage and amount per hour type. | Earning | Day overtime = (S / D) x 1.25 x H; night overtime = (S / D) x 1.75 x H; on an obligatory rest day add the art. 179 premium (+80% to 30 Jun 2026, +90% from 1 Jul 2026). D = 220 to 14 Jul 2026 / 210 from 15 Jul 2026 | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | No | Devengados / HED (NIE074-078), HEN (NIE079-083), HEDDF (NIE089-093), HENDF (NIE099-103) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

Fully taxable - there is no preferential rate and no exempt overtime allowance in Colombia. CST art. 168 num. 4 is critical: each premium 'se produce de manera exclusiva, es decir, sin acumularse con ningun otro', so the night, day-overtime and night-overtime premiums are ALTERNATIVES; only the art. 179 rest-day premium is added to them. Volume limit: 2 hours per day and 12 per week (CST art. 22 of Ley 50 de 1990 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 13), so 56 total weekly hours to 14 July 2026 and 54 from 15 July. Ministry authorisation was abolished by Ley 2466 art. 12 and replaced by a mandatory supplementary-work REGISTER; failure to keep it lets inspectors suspend all overtime for six months. Reported with quantity, start and end time, percentage and amount per hour type. | Earning | Day overtime = (S / D) x 1.25 x H; night overtime = (S / D) x 1.75 x H; on an obligatory rest day add the art. 179 premium (+80% to 30 Jun 2026, +90% from 1 Jul 2026). R98| 62140 | Salary addition | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | A recurring cash allowance of a remunerative character is salary under CST art. 127 and enters all three bases and the vacation base. Only two routes take it out of the IBC: an EXPRESS WRITTEN art. 128 non-salary pact over a genuine benefit or allowance (subject to the 40% clamp, no tax effect), or the art. 128 exclusion for amounts given to enable the work. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Recurring cash allowance / salary supplement per contract; SALARY where it is consideration for the service | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) or OtroConcepto - ConceptoS (NIE147) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R108| 62150 | Premium pay (i.e. Shift premium) | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | THE NIGHT WINDOW MOVED. CST art. 160 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 10: day work is 06:00-19:00 and night work is 19:00-06:00. Art. 160 par. 2 delayed entry into force by six months from the 25 June 2025 sanction, so the 19:00 boundary has applied SINCE 25 DECEMBER 2025 and governs the whole of 2026 - two extra hours a day now attract the 35% premium compared with the pre-reform 21:00 boundary. The night premium is salary, fully contributory, and UNLIKE overtime it STAYS IN THE VACATION BASE (CST art. 192 excludes only rest-day work and supplementary overtime hours). No night premium and no rest-day premium apply on the 6-hour / 36-hour-week successive shift system (CST arts. 161(d) and 168 num. 1). | Earning | Recargo nocturno = (S / D) x 0.35 x H for hours in the 19:00-06:00 window; rest-day / holiday premium = (S / D) x 0.80 x H to 30 Jun 2026 and x 0.90 from 1 Jul 2026. R114| 62160 | Holiday Allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | There is NO statutory vacation bonus in Colombia - only the 15 working days of paid vacation and the prima de servicios. An extra-statutory prima de vacaciones is contractual: SALARY where it is habitual (CST art. 127 'primas, sobresueldos, bonificaciones habituales'), and therefore contributory and in every base, UNLESS the parties have expressly agreed IN WRITING that it does not constitute salary under art. 128, in which case reclassify to CO-BONIF-NOSALARIAL. Configured here as the conservative default - salary. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Prima de vacaciones extralegal - a contractual amount paid on taking leave (commonly a proportion of one month's salary); no statutory entitlement exists | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Primas - PagoNS (NIE119) or Bonificacion (NIE139 / NIE140) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Contractual bonus (performance, sign-on, retention, referral) per the scheme; SALARY where habitual, non-salary only under an express written CST art. 128 pact | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Bonificacion - BonificacionS (NIE139) / BonificacionNS (NIE140) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R126| 62202 | Bonus (annual, referral, etc) | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Non-statutory bonuses are entirely contractual. They are SALARY if habitual - CST art. 127 expressly lists 'primas, sobresueldos, bonificaciones habituales' - and therefore contributory and in every base, UNLESS the parties have expressly agreed in writing that they do not constitute salary, subject always to the 40% clamp. Configured here as the conservative default - salary. Tax: no preferential bonus rate, no separate annualisation and no tax-free bonus allowance exists; the bonus enters the ordinary monthly base under both procedures (only the STATUTORY MINIMUM prima de servicios gets the separate art. 385 table read). R154| 62300 | Annual leave pay | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | VACATION IS NOT A STATUTORY BENEFIT - IT IS PAID REST, and the consequences are counter-intuitive and must be configured deliberately. Vacation pay ENTERS THE IBC (contributions continue during leave, PILA change event VAC/LR) and ENTERS THE PAYROLL-LEVY BASE, because Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 expressly covers 'los verificados por descansos remunerados de ley y convencionales o contractuales'. Its own calculation base excludes overtime and rest-day work ONLY (CST art. 192), so the night premium and commissions STAY IN. The auxilio de transporte is NOT in the vacation base. An ALL-INCLUSIVE SALARY DOES NOT ABSORB VACATION (CST art. 132 num. 2 expressly excepts it), so vacation is owed on the FULL 100% while contributions run on 70%. Fully taxable. | Earning | Vacaciones = (ordinary salary EXCLUDING overtime and rest-day work) x working days of vacation / 30; variable pay -> average of the immediately preceding year; accrual 15 working days / 360 | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Paid Rest | Yes | NA | Devengados / VacacionesComunes (NIE109-NIE112) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R157| 62310 | Expat allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Colombia applies NO separate expatriate contribution regime: a resident or non-resident foreign national employed under a Colombian contract carries FULL health, pension, occupational-risk and levy obligations at the ordinary rates. THE ONE DIVERGENCE IS TAX, AND IT IS THE SINGLE LARGEST CONFIGURATION RISK IN COLOMBIAN EXPATRIATE PAYROLL: until tax residence is acquired the withholding is a FLAT 20% ON THE GROSS under ET art. 408 with no depuration, no 25% exemption and no tax-free band, switching PROSPECTIVELY to the art. 383 table from the payment following the 183rd day of presence (DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026, which rejected applying art. 383 from day one in anticipation of residence). On COP 8,000,000 that is COP 1,600,000 of withholding against COP 92,049 for a resident - a 17-fold difference. ONE PENSION RELIEF EXISTS: a foreign employee who PROVES affiliation to a pension system abroad may opt out of Colombian pension affiliation on written election with evidence (Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.1.2.1.4; PILA field T2R2-007 'extranjero no obligado a cotizar a pensiones'). | Earning | Expatriate / assignment allowance per the assignment letter; taxable and contributory on the same rates as a local employee | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) or OtroConcepto (NIE147) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R158| 62311 | Arrears - Expat allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Colombia applies NO separate expatriate contribution regime: a resident or non-resident foreign national employed under a Colombian contract carries FULL health, pension, occupational-risk and levy obligations at the ordinary rates. THE ONE DIVERGENCE IS TAX, AND IT IS THE SINGLE LARGEST CONFIGURATION RISK IN COLOMBIAN EXPATRIATE PAYROLL: until tax residence is acquired the withholding is a FLAT 20% ON THE GROSS under ET art. 408 with no depuration, no 25% exemption and no tax-free band, switching PROSPECTIVELY to the art. 383 table from the payment following the 183rd day of presence (DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026, which rejected applying art. 383 from day one in anticipation of residence). On COP 8,000,000 that is COP 1,600,000 of withholding against COP 92,049 for a resident - a 17-fold difference. ONE PENSION RELIEF EXISTS: a foreign employee who PROVES affiliation to a pension system abroad may opt out of Colombian pension affiliation on written election with evidence (Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.1.2.1.4; PILA field T2R2-007 'extranjero no obligado a cotizar a pensiones'). | Earning | Expatriate / assignment allowance per the assignment letter; taxable and contributory on the same rates as a local employee | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) or OtroConcepto (NIE147) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

THE ONE DIVERGENCE IS TAX, AND IT IS THE SINGLE LARGEST CONFIGURATION RISK IN COLOMBIAN EXPATRIATE PAYROLL: until tax residence is acquired the withholding is a FLAT 20% ON THE GROSS under ET art. 408 with no depuration, no 25% exemption and no tax-free band, switching PROSPECTIVELY to the art. 383 table from the payment following the 183rd day of presence (DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026, which rejected applying art. 383 from day one in anticipation of residence). On COP 8,000,000 that is COP 1,600,000 of withholding against COP 92,049 for a resident - a 17-fold difference. R177| 62400 | Housing allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | A cash housing allowance is ordinary salary under CST art. 127 and enters every base. Housing supplied IN KIND is salary in kind under art. 129 - 'alimentacion, habitacion o vestuario que el empleador suministra al trabajador o a su familia' - which 'debe valorarse expresamente en todo contrato de trabajo', with expert appraisal in default of agreement, and which may not exceed 50% of total salary, or 30% where the employee earns the legal minimum wage. Where the parties have an EXPRESS WRITTEN art. 128 non-salary pact over housing, reclassify to CO-BONIF-NOSALARIAL. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Cash housing / rent allowance per contract; employer-provided housing in kind is salary in kind valued contractually, capped at 50% of total salary (30% at the SMMLV) | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Cash housing / rent allowance per contract; employer-provided housing in kind is salary in kind valued contractually, capped at 50% of total salary (30% at the SMMLV) | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R188| 62431 | Arrears - Housing & Rent allowance 1 | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | A cash housing allowance is ordinary salary under CST art. 127 and enters every base. Housing supplied IN KIND is salary in kind under art. 129 - 'alimentacion, habitacion o vestuario que el empleador suministra al trabajador o a su familia' - which 'debe valorarse expresamente en todo contrato de trabajo', with expert appraisal in default of agreement, and which may not exceed 50% of total salary, or 30% where the employee earns the legal minimum wage. Where the parties have an EXPRESS WRITTEN art. 128 non-salary pact over housing, reclassify to CO-BONIF-NOSALARIAL. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. A COMPANY CAR provided in kind for private use is instead salary in kind under art. 129, valued CONTRACTUALLY - Colombia has NO published company-car valuation scale, and art. 129 num. 2 routes an unvalued benefit to expert appraisal (estimacion pericial), not to a table. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Cash car / fuel / maintenance allowance per contract | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R207| 62510 | Car & Transportation allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | THE COUNTER-INTUITIVE COLOMBIAN LINE. The statutory transport allowance is NOT salary and is therefore OUT of the IBC and OUT of the Ley 21/1982 payroll-levy base - but it IS in the statutory-benefit base, so it enters the prima de servicios, the cesantias and the interest on them. It is NOT in the vacation base. It is taxable income (usually within the 0% band). Not payable for days of unpaid leave, sickness incapacity or maternity / paternity leave. Prorated on the 30-day commercial month. A teleworker earning less than 2 SMMLV receives the auxilio de conectividad INSTEAD, never both (Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 53). | Earning | Auxilio de transporte = 249,095 x days worked / 30, where the employee earns up to 2 SMMLV (3,501,810) and public transport is available (Decreto 1470 de 2025 art. 1) | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | No | No | No | No | No | Non-Salary | Yes | No | Devengados / Transporte - AuxilioTransporte (NIE071) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R227| 62570 | Per diem allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | CST art. 130, verbatim: 'Los viaticos permanentes constituyen salario en aquella parte destinada a proporcionar al trabajador manutencion y alojamiento; pero no en lo que solo tenga por finalidad proporcionar los medios de transporte o los gastos de representacion. Siempre que se paguen debe especificarse el valor de cada uno de estos conceptos. Los viaticos accidentales no constituyen salario en ningun caso.' The value of EACH concept must therefore be specified on payment, and the nomina electronica enforces the split with PAIRED elements ViaticoManuAlojS (NIE072) and ViaticoManuAlojNS (NIE073). Configure two wage types, not one - see CO-VIATICOS-PERMANENTES and CO-VIATICOS-OCASIONALES. | Earning | Viaticos: the part destined to MANUTENCION and ALOJAMIENTO is salary; the part for TRANSPORT and REPRESENTATION EXPENSES is not. R228| 62590 | Birth and Marriage Allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | An occasional, purely gratuitous payment is excluded from salary by CST art. 128 num. 1, so it is out of the statutory-benefit base and the vacation base - but it is FULLY TAXABLE employment income, and being a 'pago laboral no constitutivo de salario' it is counted in the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp test. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | One-off birth / marriage payment (mera liberalidad) | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes/No | Yes/No | Yes/No | No | Yes/No | Non-Salary | No | No | Devengados / Bonificacion (BonificacionNS, NIE140) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R231| 62610 | Medical insurance / Medical Care | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Employer-paid prepaid health is NOT a statutory exclusion in Colombia - it is a TAXABLE benefit to the employee, reported as an earnings line and withheld in the ordinary monthly base. R250| 62660 | Personal Allowance 1 | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | A recurring cash allowance of a remunerative character is salary under CST art. 127 and enters all three bases and the vacation base. Only two routes take it out of the IBC: an EXPRESS WRITTEN art. 128 non-salary pact over a genuine benefit or allowance (subject to the 40% clamp, no tax effect), or the art. 128 exclusion for amounts given to enable the work. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Recurring cash allowance / salary supplement per contract; SALARY where it is consideration for the service | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) or OtroConcepto - ConceptoS (NIE147) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R253| 62670 | Benefits plan 1 | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | A benefits-plan value delivered as a habitual or occasional BENEFIT OR ALLOWANCE (food, housing, clothing) can validly be pacted non-salary in writing under CST art. 128 - the Colombian workhorse. Three tests for a valid pact: it must be (a) EXPRESS, (b) WRITTEN, and (c) attached to a benefit or allowance, not to consideration for the service itself. A 'non-salary' label on what is plainly remuneration for work will not survive a UGPP contribution audit. A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Employer-funded flexible-benefits value, typically structured as non-salary under a written CST art. 128 pact | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes/No | Yes/No | Yes/No | No | Yes/No | Non-Salary | No | No | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioNS, NIE142) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R267| 62770 | Recognition Award | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Non-statutory bonuses are entirely contractual. They are SALARY if habitual - CST art. 127 expressly lists 'primas, sobresueldos, bonificaciones habituales' - and therefore contributory and in every base, UNLESS the parties have expressly agreed in writing that they do not constitute salary, subject always to the 40% clamp. Configured here as the conservative default - salary. Tax: no preferential bonus rate, no separate annualisation and no tax-free bonus allowance exists; the bonus enters the ordinary monthly base under both procedures (only the STATUTORY MINIMUM prima de servicios gets the separate art. 385 table read). A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Contractual bonus (performance, sign-on, retention, referral) per the scheme; SALARY where habitual, non-salary only under an express written CST art. 128 pact | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Bonificacion - BonificacionS (NIE139) / BonificacionNS (NIE140) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R277| 62880 | Post-termination payment | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Termination payments are FULLY TAXABLE ordinary employment income entering the ordinary monthly base, and are OUT of the IBC and the payroll-levy base. Colombia has NO concessional cap on termination payments: only the statutory-minimum indemnities of ET art. 206 nums. 1-3 are exempt and even those only up to the legal minimum amount (art. 206 par. 1). A negotiated mutual-termination bonus and a payment in lieu of notice are frequently and WRONGLY assumed exempt - they are not. Employees with 10 or more years of continuous service at the entry into force of Ley 789 de 2002 retain the earlier Ley 50 de 1990 art. 6 scale. Note the CST art. 65 par. 1 gate: the employer must evidence payment of the last three months' contributions within 60 days of the termination, failing which the termination HAS NO EFFECT. For a NON-RESIDENT this is withheld at the flat 20% on the gross under ET art. 408 with no depuration (DIAN Concepto 011108 de 2026); the switch to the art. 383 table is prospective from the payment following the 183rd day of presence. | Earning | Indemnizacion CST art. 64: below 10 SMMLV -> 30 days' salary for the first year + 20 days per subsequent year and pro rata; at or above 10 SMMLV (17,509,050) -> 20 days + 15 days | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | No | No | No | No | No | Non-Salary | No | No | Devengados / Indemnizacion (NIE160); BonifRetiro (NIE159) for a settlement bonus | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R280| 62883 | Post-termination payment | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Termination payments are FULLY TAXABLE ordinary employment income entering the ordinary monthly base, and are OUT of the IBC and the payroll-levy base. Colombia has NO concessional cap on termination payments: only the statutory-minimum indemnities of ET art. 206 nums. 1-3 are exempt and even those only up to the legal minimum amount (art. 206 par. 1). A negotiated mutual-termination bonus and a payment in lieu of notice are frequently and WRONGLY assumed exempt - they are not. Employees with 10 or more years of continuous service at the entry into force of Ley 789 de 2002 retain the earlier Ley 50 de 1990 art. 6 scale. Note the CST art. 65 par. 1 gate: the employer must evidence payment of the last three months' contributions within 60 days of the termination, failing which the termination HAS NO EFFECT. R281| 62890 | Stock options and grants | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Equity and share-incentive awards are TAXABLE EMPLOYMENT INCOME AT EXERCISE OR VESTING, on the spread, entering the ordinary monthly base and the ET art. 383 table. The subsequent disposal is a separate capital transaction outside payroll. Generally non-salary (an occasional, non-consideration benefit) so out of the statutory-benefit and vacation bases, but A valid written CST art. 128 non-salary pact keeps this out of the IBC ONLY up to the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp; the excess over 40% of total remuneration is forced BACK into the health, pension, solidarity-fund and occupational-risk base while remaining non-salary for the statutory benefits. The clamp does NOT reach the CCF/SENA/ICBF levy base, which has its own Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 definition. A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. For a non-resident, ET art. 408 applies at 20% where the underlying service was performed in Colombia; where the service was performed abroad the income is foreign-source and not taxable. | Earning | Taxable spread = market value at exercise or vesting less the exercise price paid | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes/No | Yes/No | Yes/No | No | Yes/No | Non-Salary | No | No | Devengados / OtroConcepto (NIE146-NIE148) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R282| 62900 | Statutory Gratuity, EOS | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Cesantias and the interest on them are STATUTORY BENEFITS excluded from salary by CST art. 128, so out of the IBC and out of the payroll-levy base. They are EXCLUDED FROM THE MONTHLY WITHHOLDING BASE ENTIRELY under BOTH procedures (ET arts. 385 and 386) - not merely exempt - and the ET art. 206 num. 4 exemption is applied when the payment is made: exempt in full where the average monthly income of the last 6 months is <= 350 UVT (18,330,900), then tapering 90/80/60/40/20/0%. The 25% exemption of art. 206 num. 10 does NOT apply (art. 206 par. 2). Liquidated at 31 December and DEPOSITED TO THE EMPLOYEE'S FUND BY 14 FEBRUARY; late deposit costs one day's salary per day of delay. R283| 62901 | Supplemental basic (i.e. 13th or 14th months salary) | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | The statutory service bonus is Colombia's 13th-month equivalent. CST art. 307: 'La prima anual no es salario, ni se computara como factor del salario en ningun caso' - so it is OUT of the IBC and OUT of the payroll-levy base. TAX TREATMENT DIVERGES SHARPLY BY PROCEDURE and is one of the most commonly mis-implemented rules in Colombian payroll: under PROCEDURE 1 the statutory minimum bonus is withheld SEPARATELY on its own read of the art. 383 table (ET art. 385) and is NOT added to the month's salary; under PROCEDURE 2 it is folded into the ordinary monthly base, the 13 divisor of art. 386 having already prorated its effect. Any excess above the statutory minimum goes to the ordinary monthly base under both. Contractual and Christmas bonuses are set off against it (CST art. 308). Absorbed by an all-inclusive salary. | Earning | Prima de servicios = (monthly salary + auxilio de transporte) x days in the semester / 360, i.e. 30 days' salary per year - half by 30 June, half by 20 December | CO-TX-03 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | No | No | No | No | No | Non-Salary | NA | NA | Devengados / Primas - Cantidad (NIE117), Pago (NIE118), PagoNS (NIE119) | ET arts. 385 and 386; CST arts. 306 (as amended by Ley 1788 de 2016 art. 1), 307 and 308. | Yes

A non-salary pact has NO tax effect whatsoever. | Earning | Recurring cash allowance / salary supplement per contract; SALARY where it is consideration for the service | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioS, NIE141) or OtroConcepto - ConceptoS (NIE147) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R309| CO-RECARGO-DOMINICAL | Obligatory rest-day / holiday premium (80% / 90%) | Recargo Dominical y Festivo (80% hasta 30-jun-2026 / 90% desde 1-jul-2026) | CST art. 179 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 14, transitional paragraph verified verbatim: '...80%. A partir del primero de julio de 2026, se incrementara el recargo por laborar en dia de descanso obligatorio a 90%. A partir del primero de julio de 2027, se dara plena aplicacion al recargo ... sin perjuicio de que ... el empleador se acoja al recargo del 100%.' THE THREE-VALUE STRUCTURE (MinTrabajo Circular 101 de 2025 SS4): working an obligatory rest day generates THREE independent payment obligations - (1) the paid rest day itself at one day's ordinary salary (CST art. 173), (2) the hours actually worked, (3) this additional premium. An engine that pays only the hours plus the premium UNDERPAYS BY ONE DAY. Occasional = up to 2 rest days in a calendar month; 3 or more is habitual and gives the employee the choice of a compensatory rest day or the remuneration (arts. 179 par. 1, 180). OUT of the vacation base. TWO 2026 CUT-OVERS TWO WEEKS APART: this premium steps on 1 JULY, the 42-hour week and the 210 divisor on 15 JULY - never collapse them. A DEFECT RECORDED FOR TRACEABILITY: Circular 101 SS5.1 states a 75% premium (the pre-reform figure) after correctly stating the 80/90/100 phase-in in SS4; the SS4 schedule governs and the 75% reference must not be configured. Likewise the electronic-payroll Technical Annex v1.0 hard-codes the pre-reform 100/75/150/110 percentages - DIAN Concepto 013840 (int. 1568) de 1 octubre 2025 resolves that the annex percentage 'must be read in accordance with the rules in force for each specific case', so the engine reports the real statutory percentage. | Earning | (S / D) x 0.80 x H to 30 June 2026; x 0.90 from 1 July 2026; x 1.00 from 1 July 2027 - IN ADDITION TO the paid rest day itself and the hours actually worked | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | No | Devengados / HRDDF - Cantidad (NIE096), Porcentaje (NIE097), Pago (NIE098) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes

R314| CO-COMISIONES | Commissions and sales percentages | Comisiones y Porcentajes sobre Ventas | SALARY BY EXPRESS STATUTORY DEFINITION - CST art. 127 names 'porcentajes sobre ventas y comisiones'. In the IBC, the payroll-levy base, the statutory-benefit base AND THE VACATION BASE (art. 192 excludes only rest-day work and supplementary overtime hours, so commissions remain). Not prorated for a part month - paid as earned. Fully taxable. A large commission month can push the employee across the 4-SMMLV solidarity-fund trigger (7,003,620), across the 10-SMMLV exoneration line (17,509,050) or into a higher withholding band, all tested on that month's actual figures. R315| CO-BONIF-HABITUAL | Habitual bonus (salary) | Bonificacion Habitual (Salarial) | CST art. 127 brings 'bonificaciones habituales' INTO salary, so a habitual bonus without a written non-salary pact enters every base. The habituality test is the audit risk: a payment repeated year on year will not survive a UGPP contribution audit as an occasional gratuity. R316| CO-BONIF-NOSALARIAL | Extra-statutory bonus under a written non-salary pact | Bonificacion Extralegal No Salarial (Pacto CST art. 128) | THE COLOMBIAN WORKHOUSE AND ITS LIMIT. Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30, verified verbatim: 'Sin perjuicio de lo previsto para otros fines, para los efectos relacionados con los articulos 18 y 204 de la Ley 100 de 1993, los pagos laborales no constitutivos de salario de los trabajadores particulares no podran ser superiores al 40% del total de la remuneracion.' A valid pact removes the payment from the labour-law salary base PERMANENTLY - it never enters the statutory-benefit base or the overtime base - but removes it from the CONTRIBUTION base only up to 40%. The excess is added back for health, pension, the solidarity fund and occupational risk while remaining non-salary for the benefits: TWO DIVERGENT BASES, PERMANENTLY. The clamp reaches arts. 18 and 204 of Ley 100 ONLY - it does not reach the CCF / SENA / ICBF levies, which have their own Ley 21/1982 base. AND IT HAS NO TAX EFFECT WHATSOEVER: the payment remains fully taxable employment income. Any engine that treats a non-salary flag as a tax exclusion will systematically under-withhold. See also Resolucion MinProteccion 2641 de 2011. | Earning | Contractual amount under an EXPRESS WRITTEN non-salary pact; excluded from the IBC only up to 40% of total remuneration (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30), the excess forced back in | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes/No | Yes/No | Yes/No | No | Yes/No | Non-Salary | No | No | Devengados / Bonificacion - BonificacionNS (NIE140) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R317| CO-GRATIF-OCASIONAL | Occasional gratuity (mera liberalidad) | Gratificacion Ocasional | CST art. 128 excludes 'las sumas que ocasionalmente y por mera liberalidad recibe el trabajador del empleador' from salary, so out of the statutory-benefit and vacation bases - but FULLY TAXABLE, and counted in the 40% clamp test as a pago laboral no constitutivo de salario. | Earning | One-off gratuitous amount, not habitual and not contractually owed | CO-TX-01 | Lump Sum |  | Additional | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes/No | Yes/No | Yes/No | No | Yes/No | Non-Salary | No | No | Devengados / Bonificacion - BonificacionNS (NIE140) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R318| CO-SALARIO-ESPECIE | Salary in kind | Salario en Especie | CST art. 129, verified verbatim: 'Constituye salario en especie toda aquella parte de la remuneracion ordinaria y permanente que reciba el trabajador como contraprestacion directa del servicio, tales como alimentacion, habitacion o vestuario que el empleador suministra al trabajador o a su familia... El salario en especie debe valorarse expresamente en todo contrato de trabajo. A falta de estipulacion o de acuerdo sobre su valor real se estimara pericialmente, sin que pueda llegar a constituir y conformar mas del cincuenta por ciento (50%) de la totalidad del salario. No obstante, cuando un trabajador devengue el salario minimo legal, el valor por el concepto de salario en especie no podra exceder del treinta por ciento (30%).' Fully in the IBC, the payroll-levy base, the statutory-benefit base and the vacation base, and fully taxable. COLOMBIA HAS NO PUBLISHED BENEFIT-IN-KIND VALUATION SCALE - no company-car table, no accommodation scale, no notional-value list; the absence is principled, because art. 129 num. 2 routes an unvalued benefit to expert appraisal, not to a table. The engine therefore REQUIRES a contractual value on every in-kind wage type. There is no fringe-benefits tax and no separate benefits return. Mechanically the benefit produces a positive earnings line plus an offsetting deduction line, so net cash pay is unchanged while the withholding and contributions are correct. | Earning | Value stated EXPRESSLY IN THE EMPLOYMENT CONTRACT, or by expert appraisal in default of agreement; may not exceed 50% of total salary, or 30% where the employee earns the legal minimum wage | CO-TX-01 | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Salary | Yes | Yes | Devengados / OtroConcepto - ConceptoS (NIE147) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). | Yes
R319| CO-PRIMA-SERVICIOS | Statutory service bonus | Prima de Servicios | CST art. 306 as amended by Ley 1788 de 2016 art. 1, universal for every private-sector employee and expressly including domestic-service employees, family-service drivers, day workers and farm workers. CST art. 307: 'La prima anual no es salario, ni se computara como factor del salario en ningun caso' - so OUT of the IBC and OUT of the payroll-levy base. Base = salary PLUS the auxilio de transporte; variable pay enters at the semester average. Set off against contractual or Christmas bonuses (CST art. 308), so an employer already paying a 13th-month equivalent does not pay twice. Absorbed by an all-inclusive salary. R320| CO-CESANTIAS | Severance savings | Auxilio de Cesantias | Statutory benefit under CST Titles VIII-IX, excluded from salary by art. 128, so out of the IBC and the payroll-levy base. EXCLUDED FROM THE MONTHLY WITHHOLDING BASE ENTIRELY under both procedures (ET arts. 385, 386) - not merely exempt. Exempt in full where the average monthly income of the last six months is <= 350 UVT (18,330,900), then tapering 90 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0% through 650 UVT (ET art. 206 num. 4); the 25% exemption of art. 206 num. 10 does NOT apply (art. 206 par. 2). LATE DEPOSIT COSTS ONE DAY'S SALARY PER DAY OF DELAY (Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99 num. 3). Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 adds a paragraph to Ley 50 art. 99 permitting the employer to deposit 8.33% of the base monthly as an ADVANCE DEPOSIT - verified verbatim: 'En ningun caso la consignacion anticipada podra entenderse como pago parcial de la cesantia, pues el trabajador solo tendra acceso a la cesantia anticipada en los terminos que asi lo determine la Ley o a la terminacion del contrato de trabajo.' R322| CO-VACACIONES | Annual leave taken | Vacaciones Disfrutadas | VACATION IS NOT A PRESTACION SOCIAL - IT IS PAID REST, and every consequence is counter-intuitive. It ENTERS THE IBC (contributions continue during leave; PILA change event VAC/LR) and ENTERS THE PAYROLL-LEVY BASE (Ley 21/1982 art. 17 covers descansos remunerados by name). Its own base excludes overtime and rest-day work ONLY - CST art. 192, verified verbatim: 'solo se excluiran para la liquidacion de vacaciones el valor del trabajo en dias de descanso obligatorio y el valor del trabajo suplementario en horas extras' - so the NIGHT PREMIUM AND COMMISSIONS STAY IN. The auxilio de transporte is NOT in the vacation base. AN ALL-INCLUSIVE SALARY DOES NOT ABSORB IT (CST art. 132 num. 2 expressly excepts vacation), so vacation is owed on the FULL 100% while contributions run on 70%. Fully taxable. R335| CO-APOYO-APRENDIZ | Apprentice maintenance support | Apoyo de Sostenimiento del Aprendiz | CST art. 81 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21, verified verbatim: 'Si es una formacion dual, el aprendiz recibira como minimo durante el primer ano el equivalente al setenta y cinco por ciento (75%) de un (1) salario minimo legal mensual vigente y durante el segundo ano el equivalente al cien por ciento (100%)... NOT the minimum wage as such - a maintenance support payment expressed as a percentage of it. SPECIAL CONTRIBUTION REGIME: Decreto 0223 de 5 marzo 2026 art. 2.2.6.3.3.4 par. 2 provides that 'en todo caso la base de cotizacion debe ser [de] un (1) salario minimo legal mensual vigente', so an apprentice on 75% of the SMMLV nonetheless contributes on a FULL minimum wage. FULLY TAXABLE ordinary employment income entering the ordinary monthly base - ET art. 206 nums. 1-3 exempt only accident, illness and maternity-protection indemnities and art. 206 par. 1 limits even those to the statutory minimum amount, so an art. 64 indemnity has NO tax-free portion and Colombia has NO concessional cap on termination payments. R355| CO-ANTICIPO-NOMINA | Payroll advance (accrued) | Anticipo de Nomina (Devengado) | CONDITIONAL BY DESIGN. R356| CO-REINTEGRO | Reimbursement to the employee (accrued) | Reintegro (Devengado) | A reimbursement is not remuneration and is outside every base. R361| CO-RETENCION-FUENTE | Income tax withholding on work income (resident) | Retencion en la Fuente sobre Rentas de Trabajo | THE ONLY PAYROLL WITHHOLDING IN COLOMBIA - there is no employer payroll tax on income and no local, municipal, departmental, church or solidarity surcharge on employment income. NON-CUMULATIVE: each month stands alone under Procedure 1, and there is NO employer year-end true-up of any kind - the employee reconciles in their own annual return. TWO ORDERING RULES DOMINATE: (1) the 25% exempt income of art. 206 num. 10 is computed LAST, on the figure net of non-taxable income, the art. 387 deductions and other exempt income - computing 25% of GROSS overstates it and under-withholds; (2) the 72-UVT-per-dependant deduction of art. 336 num. 3 sits OUTSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps. Caps: (D+E) <= 40% of (C = A - B) AND <= 1,340 UVT/year (5,848,430/month as apportioned), with the 25% exemption itself capped at 790 UVT/year (3,447,955/month). CESANTIAS AND THEIR INTEREST NEVER ENTER THE MONTHLY BASE under either procedure. A SUB-30-DAY PERIOD IS A PERCENTAGE RULE, NOT AN AMOUNT RULE: divide the payments by the days, multiply by 30, read the table PERCENTAGE, then apply that percentage to the actual smaller payment (art. 385) - reading the table for an AMOUNT on the grossed-up figure would grossly over-withhold. The employee may request a HIGHER rate in writing, effective from the month AFTER the request (art. 383 par. 3); it overrides upward only, never downward. Where an identifier or an employee declaration is missing there is NO penalty rate - the employee simply loses the deduction and is over-withheld. | Tax | PROCEDURE 1 (ET art. 385): read the art. 383 table on the month's art. 388 depurated base - 0% to 95 UVT (4,975,530), then 19% / 28% / 33% / 35% / 37% / 39% above 95 / 150 / 360 / 640 / 945 / 2,300 UVT. PROCEDURE 2 (art. 386): fixed % = table(sum of the preceding 12 months' taxable payments / 13), recomputed each JUNE and DECEMBER and applied to the month's depurated base | CO-NA | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Deducciones / RetencionFuente (NIE177); Formulario 350 casillas 77 and 93 | N/A - mechanism line. R362| CO-RETENCION-NO-RESIDENTE | Income tax withholding - non-resident (flat 20%) | Retencion en la Fuente a No Residentes (20% - ET art. 408) | ET art. 408. THE SINGLE LARGEST CONFIGURATION RISK IN COLOMBIAN EXPATRIATE PAYROLL: on COP 8,000,000 the withholding is COP 1,600,000 against COP 92,049 for a resident - a 17-fold difference on the same salary. THE RESIDENCE SWITCH IS PROSPECTIVE, NEVER RETROACTIVE: DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026, upholding Concepto 1789 int. 14595 de 2025, rejected the argument that a foreign employee on a contract exceeding 183 days may be withheld under art. 383 from day one in anticipation of future residence. Withholding is determined AT THE MOMENT OF EACH PAYMENT on the status then held, and the switch to the art. 383 table takes effect FROM THE PAYMENT FOLLOWING the date the 183-day test of art. 10 is met, with NO true-up of the earlier months through payroll. Where the service is performed ENTIRELY OUTSIDE COLOMBIA by a non-resident the income is foreign-source and there is NO WITHHOLDING AT ALL (DIAN Oficio 28380 de 2019). Non-residents are IN SCOPE for Mercans Colombia payroll - a foreign national employed under a Colombian contract before acquiring residence is a common case - so the engine must carry a residence flag with an effective date and support the mid-year switch. Treaty relief is a CLIENT DECISION requiring a residence certificate and is applied only on written client instruction. | Tax | gross payment x 20%, with NO art. 388 depuration, NO art. 387 deductions, NO 25% exempt income and NO tax-free band - 20% from the first peso | CO-NA | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Deducciones / RetencionFuente (NIE177); Formulario 350 casillas 109-112 and hoja 2 casillas 141-155 | N/A - mechanism line. R372| CO-DED-EMBARGO | Judicial or tax attachment | Embargo Judicial o Fiscal | CST art. 154 makes salary UNATTACHABLE as the general rule; art. 155 opens only the excess over the minimum wage, and only to one fifth of that excess. A TAX LEVY BY DIAN counts as a court order for CST art. 149 purposes because the tax authority holds jurisdictional powers under ET arts. 823 et seq. and may issue an attachment order without going to court; UGPP social-security enforcement is treated the same way. Competing ordinary attachments share the art. 155 headroom IN DATE ORDER OF SERVICE on the employer. WHERE COMPETING ORDERS EXCEED THE CEILING the employer applies them in priority to the cap and NOTIFIES the court or authority whose order cannot be satisfied, stating the competing orders and the amounts applied - it must NOT apportion pro rata on its own initiative and must NOT exceed the ceiling to satisfy all orders. R373| CO-DED-ALIMENTOS | Maintenance / child-support garnishment | Embargo por Alimentos | CST art. 156 is the one broad exception to Colombia's otherwise robust salary protection: for MAINTENANCE obligations and debts owed to COOPERATIVES, up to half the whole salary is attachable, minimum wage included. Maintenance ranks FIRST among garnishments - the obligation is of constitutional rank (Political Constitution art. 44, the prevailing rights of the child) - and it may lawfully take net pay BELOW the minimum wage, which no voluntary deduction ever can. Served by judicial order from a family judge, or a conciliation agreement approved by a family commissioner or the ICBF, on receipt of which the employer becomes a RETAINING AGENT and must acknowledge the order, apply it from the next payroll falling due after service, remit to the specified account, report any termination, unpaid leave or salary change affecting it, and keep the order and remittance evidence for the retention period. THE TWO CEILINGS ARE NOT CUMULATIVE ALLOWANCES: art. 156 (50% of the whole) and art. 155 (20% of the excess) are different ceilings for different claim types - apply the maintenance order first within its own 50% ceiling, then the commercial attachment only to the extent that the art. 155 headroom on the excess over the SMMLV remains unused. | Deduction | Up to 50% OF THE WHOLE SALARY, INCLUDING THE MINIMUM-WAGE PORTION - CST art. 156 | CO-NA | Pro Rata |  | Recurring | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Deducciones / EmbargoFiscal (NIE181) or PagosTerceros (NIE195) | N/A - mechanism line. R376| CO-ER-SALUD | Health contribution (employer) 8.5% | Aporte a Salud - Empleador (8,5%) | Ley 100 de 1993 art. 204 (total 12.5%). THE EXONERATION IS TESTED PER EMPLOYEE, PER MONTH, on what the employee devenga that month, so an overtime or commission month can lose it, and where a mid-month increase takes the MONTH TOTAL across the line the exoneration is lost for the WHOLE month - the test is on the month, not on the segment. AT EXACTLY 10 SMMLV THE EXONERATION IS LOST, costing the employer approximately COP 2,363,800 per month and taking the employer cost factor from about 1.3836 to about 1.52 - the single largest step-change in Colombian employer cost. R387| CO-BASE-IBC | Social-security contribution base | Ingreso Base de Cotizacion (IBC) | BASE 1 OF THREE, AND THE ORDER OF THE FOUR ADJUSTMENTS MATTERS: applying the ceiling before the 40% clamp gives a different and wrong answer. Ley 100 de 1993 art. 18 as substituted by Ley 797 de 2003 art. 5. The SAME base serves health (art. 204 par. 1), pension, the solidarity fund and occupational risk (Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.3.3). ACTUAL salary, not notional - Colombia has no banded, class or scheduled contribution wage, so a variable-pay employee has a different IBC every month and can legitimately cross the 4-SMMLV solidarity trigger, the 10-SMMLV exoneration line and the 25-SMMLV ceiling in different directions in successive months. Where actual pay falls below the floor the base is RAISED to one SMMLV pro-rated to contributory days, so the employee still contributes 4% + 4% of the raised base even though the salary was lower. Note the asymmetry the engine must implement: the SMMLV floor protects NET PAY against voluntary deductions, while the IBC is SEPARATELY floored at one SMMLV - two different floors doing two different jobs. | Base | SUM salary-constituting payments (transport / connectivity allowance, prima, cesantias and interest EXCLUDED; vacation INCLUDED) -> apply the 40% clamp -> all-inclusive salary = 70% -> FLOOR 1 SMMLV (1,750,905) pro-rated to contributory days -> CEILING 25 SMMLV (43,772,625) -> round UP to the whole peso | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | PILA T2R2-042 (IBC pension), T2R2-043 (IBC salud), T2R2-044 (IBC riesgos laborales) | N/A - mechanism line. OPEN ITEM: the 790-UVT and 1,340-UVT caps are stated ANNUALLY yet withholding is monthly; market practice, which Mercans follows, apportions them at one twelfth - 65.8333 UVT = 3,447,955 and 111.6667 UVT = 5,848,430 - and tests each month against the apportioned figure.
