Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R392| CO-INT-CLAMP40 | Non-salary excess forced back into the contribution base | Excedente No Salarial Reintegrado al IBC (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30) | THE INTERMEDIARY THAT MAKES TWO BASES DIVERGE PERMANENTLY. Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30, verified verbatim against the gazette: 'Sin perjuicio de lo previsto para otros fines, para los efectos relacionados con los articulos 18 y 204 de la Ley 100 de 1993, los pagos laborales no constitutivos de salario de los trabajadores particulares no podran ser superiores al 40% del total de la remuneracion.' WORKED ILLUSTRATION (CCG Module 6.3): salary 8,000,000 plus a 20,000,000 retention bonus under a valid non-salary pact gives total remuneration 28,000,000, of which non-salary is 71.4%; permitted non-salary is 40% x 28,000,000 = 11,200,000, so 8,800,000 is FORCED BACK IN and the IBC becomes 8,000,000 + 8,800,000 = 16,800,000 - while the whole 20,000,000 remains non-salary for the prima, cesantias, interest and vacation, and fully taxable for withholding. The clamp reaches Ley 100 arts. 18 and 204 only, so it does not touch the CCF / SENA / ICBF levy base. See also Resolucion MinProteccion 2641 de 2011. UGPP audit exposure sits squarely on this line: it cross-matches tax-authority data against filed contribution returns. | Intermediary | IF total non-salary payments > 40% x total remuneration THEN excess = non-salary total - (0.40 x total remuneration), added to the IBC; ELSE 0. Computed BEFORE the 70% all-inclusive reduction, the floor and the ceiling | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Derived - feeds PILA T2R2-042, T2R2-043, T2R2-044 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R393| CO-RATE-HORA-ORDINARIA | Ordinary hour value | Valor de la Hora Ordinaria | THE SINGLE BIGGEST 2026 CALCULATION CHANGE. Circular 101 publishes the gradual path: 47h/235 from 15 July 2023, 46h/230 from 15 July 2024, 44h/220 from 15 July 2025 and 42h/210 FROM 15 JULY 2026 (Ley 2101 de 2021 art. 3; CST art. 161 as substituted by Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 11, verified verbatim: 'una jornada maxima de cuarenta y dos (42) horas a la semana', distributable by agreement over five or six days, always preserving the rest day and WITHOUT REDUCING SALARY). THE LEGACY DIVISOR OF 240 IS OBSOLETE AND UNDERSTATES EVERY PREMIUM BY ABOUT 14.3%. A JULY 2026 PAYROLL SPANS BOTH DIVISORS and must be split at 15 July - and separately at 1 July for the rest-day premium step from 80% to 90%. Two cut-overs, two weeks apart; never collapse them. Where the week is distributed over five days with Saturday and/or Sunday rest, the ordinary daily shift may be extended by up to two hours WITHOUT paying supplementary work (CST art. 164; Circular 101 SS2.1). | Percentage | monthly salary / D, where D = 220 to 14 July 2026 and 210 from 15 July 2026 (MinTrabajo Circular Externa 101 de 22 septiembre 2025). Minimum hourly value: 7,958.66 then 8,337.64 | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Derived - feeds every hour-type Porcentaje / Pago element | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R394| CO-PCT-RETENCION-PROC2 | Procedure 2 fixed withholding percentage | Porcentaje Fijo de Retencion (Procedimiento 2) | ET art. 386. THE DIVISOR IS 13, NOT 12 - art. 386 says 'dividir por 13' - because the 13 prorates the 13th-month effect of the prima de servicios, which under Procedure 2 sits INSIDE the ordinary base rather than receiving the separate art. 385 table read; using 12 over-withholds by roughly 8%. A BACKWARD-LOOKING ACCUMULATOR, NOT A PROJECTION: Colombia is not an annualised or cumulative withholding system, so there is no annual estimate, no notional annual salary, no projected bonus and no year-end catch-up. CESANTIAS AND THEIR INTEREST ARE EXPRESSLY EXCLUDED from the accumulator; everything else is included - salary, overtime, the night and rest-day premia, commissions, habitual bonuses, the prima de servicios, salary in kind, the transport allowance, vacation pay and cash-compensated vacation. Art. 387 par. 1 requires the art. 387 deductions to be used BOTH in computing the fixed percentage AND in computing the monthly base it is applied to. A new hire has no 12-month history so no fixed percentage exists: read the table on the month's own taxable payments until the first semi-annual calculation, whose divisor is then the ACTUAL months of service, not 13. A large mid-year increase produces under-withholding for the rest of the semester and THE EMPLOYER MAY NOT UNILATERALLY RAISE THE FIXED PERCENTAGE MID-SEMESTER - the employee's only remedy is a written request for a higher rate under art. 383 par. 3. | Percentage | fixed % = table(sum of the preceding 12 months' taxable payments / 13) expressed as a percentage of that average; recomputed each JUNE and DECEMBER and applied to each of the FOLLOWING SIX MONTHS' depurated bases | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Derived - feeds Deducciones / RetencionFuente (NIE177) | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R395| CO-REDONDEO | Rounding residual (electronic payroll) | Redondeo | THREE ROUNDING REGIMES COEXIST IN ONE COLOMBIAN PAYROLL MONTH AND MUST NEVER BE UNIFIED: the PILA rounds UP - the IBC to the whole peso and each contribution to the next multiple of 100 (Decreto 1990 de 2016 art. 1, substituting Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.5); the nomina electronica uses ROUND-HALF-TO-EVEN with a +/-2.00 tolerance and carries this EXPLICIT Redondeo element for the residual; and the DIAN return forms round to the NEAREST 1,000 (ET art. 577). Net pay itself is not separately rounded to any statutory rule - it is the arithmetic residue after each deduction has already been rounded under its own regime. | Notional | residual difference such that total accrued - total deducted +/- redondeo = total payable, within a tolerance of +/-2.00, using ROUND-HALF-TO-EVEN | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NominaIndividual / Redondeo (NIE186), DevengadosTotal (NIE187), DeduccionesTotal (NIE188), ComprobanteTotal (NIE189) | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R396| CO-FLAG-SALARIO-INTEGRAL | All-inclusive salary flag | Indicador de Salario Integral | A REQUIRED field of the nomina electronica (NIE056) and of the PILA (T2R2-041). Requires an ORDINARY salary above 10 SMMLV plus a benefit factor of at least 30%, agreed IN WRITING (CST art. 132 num. 2). Drives the 70% reduction of BOTH the IBC (Ley 100 art. 18 final paragraph) and the payroll-levy base (CST art. 132 num. 3: 'los aportes se disminuiran en un treinta por ciento (30%)') - but NEVER of vacation, which stays at 100% because art. 132 num. 2 expressly excepts it. | Flag | TRUE where a WRITTEN all-inclusive salary agreement exists and the salary is >= 13 SMMLV (22,761,765); drives IBC = 70% and payroll-levy base = 70%, but vacation at 100% | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Trabajador / SalarioIntegral (NIE056); Sueldo (NIE062); PILA T2R2-041 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R397| CO-FLAG-EXONERACION-114-1 | ET art. 114-1 exoneration flag | Indicador de Exoneracion de Aportes (ET art. 114-1) | TESTED PER EMPLOYEE, PER MONTH, on what the employee devenga - not on the contractual salary, and not on a segment of the month, so a mid-month increase that takes the MONTH TOTAL across the line loses the exoneration for the WHOLE month. AT EXACTLY 10 SMMLV THE EXONERATION IS LOST - the statute says 'menos de diez (10) salarios minimos', so the test is strictly less than - costing approximately COP 2,363,800 per month and taking the employer cost factor from about 1.3836 to about 1.52. PENSION 12%, OCCUPATIONAL RISK AND THE CCF 4% ARE NEVER EXONERATED. Carried on the PILA at both employer level (A1-033) and contributor level (T2R2-076), and on Formulario 350 casilla 27 for the related self-withholding configuration. | Flag | TRUE where the employer is an income-tax-filing legal person (or a natural person employing 2 or more workers) AND the employee's month earnings are STRICTLY LESS THAN 10 SMMLV (17,509,050); suppresses employer health 8.5%, SENA 2% and ICBF 3% | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | PILA A1-033, T2R2-076; Formulario 350 casilla 27 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R398| CO-FLAG-RESIDENCIA-FISCAL | Tax residence flag with effective date | Indicador de Residencia Fiscal con Fecha de Efecto | THE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL FLAG IN COLOMBIAN PAYROLL - a 17-fold withholding difference on the same salary. Must be RE-EVALUATED EVERY PAYROLL PERIOD and switched PROSPECTIVELY from the payment FOLLOWING the date the test is met; never applied retroactively through payroll (DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026). Where the 183-day period straddles two years, residence attaches to the year in which the 183rd day falls. A SECOND CONDITION SITS BESIDE IT: where the service is performed entirely outside Colombia by a non-resident the income is foreign-source and there is no withholding at all (DIAN Oficio 28380 de 2019), so the engine also needs a place-of-service flag - the nomina electronica carries LugarTrabajoPais (NIE050) for exactly this. | Flag | TRUE where the employee is a Colombian tax resident AT THE DATE OF THIS PAYMENT (ET art. 10: more than 183 calendar days, continuous or not, in any 365-day period, or the diplomatic or nationality-plus-connection tests); drives the art. 383 table versus the art. 408 flat 20% | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Trabajador / LugarTrabajoPais (NIE050); Formulario 350 hoja 2 casillas 141, 144 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R399| CO-FLAG-ALTO-RIESGO-PENSION | High-risk pension activity flag | Indicador de Actividad de Alto Riesgo Pensional | A REQUIRED field of the nomina electronica (NIE043) with a matching PILA special-pension-rate indicator (T2R2-079). The FLAG itself is mandatory and must be populated for EVERY employee, high-risk or not, or the XML fails validation. OPEN ITEM as to the 2026 additional RATE - see CO-ER-PENSION-ALTO-RIESGO and the QA report. | Flag | TRUE where the employee performs an activity listed in Decreto-Ley 2090 de 2003; drives the additional employer pension contribution and a reduced retirement age | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Trabajador / AltoRiesgoPension (NIE043); PILA T2R2-079 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R400| CO-FLAG-PACTO-NO-SALARIAL | Written non-salary pact flag | Indicador de Pacto de Exclusion Salarial (CST art. 128) | THREE TESTS FOR A VALID PACT: it must be (a) EXPRESS, (b) WRITTEN, and (c) attached to a BENEFIT OR ALLOWANCE, not to consideration for the service itself. A 'non-salary' label on what is plainly remuneration for work will not survive a UGPP contribution audit. THE FLAG IS A HARD CONFIGURATION VALUE, NOT A RUNTIME DERIVATION: the nomina electronica separates salary from non-salary AT ELEMENT LEVEL with paired S / NS element names - bonus, allowance, generic concept, generic payment, voucher payment, meal payment and travel/accommodation allowance - so the engine must carry the flag on every wage type and must not attempt to derive it at reporting time. NOTE THE TWO ASYMMETRIES: the pact removes the payment from the labour-law bases PERMANENTLY but from the IBC only up to 40%, and IT HAS NO TAX EFFECT AT ALL. Colombian benefit structuring is a social-security optimisation, never a tax optimisation; any engine that treats a non-salary flag as a tax exclusion will systematically under-withhold. THERE IS NO SALARY-SACRIFICE REGIME IN COLOMBIAN LAW - CST art. 13 makes the minimum labour rights irrenounceable. | Flag | TRUE where an EXPRESS, WRITTEN, SIGNED CST art. 128 pact exists over a genuine benefit or allowance; drives the salary / non-salary element split and the 40% clamp computation | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Drives every paired S / NS element (NIE139/140, NIE141/142, NIE147/148, NIE151/152, NIE153/154, NIE072/073) | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R401| CO-FLAG-PENSIONADO-ACTIVO | Employed old-age pensioner flag | Indicador de Pensionado en Activo | PENSION CONTRIBUTION CEASES ENTIRELY for an employed old-age pensioner - neither share is due - while health, occupational risk and the payroll levies continue. Reported with PILA worker sub-type 01. REACHING RETIREMENT AGE DOES NOT BY ITSELF STOP THE CONTRIBUTION: being a PENSIONER IN ACTIVE SERVICE does. Retirement age (57 for women, 62 for men in the public defined-benefit regime) affects entitlement and the point at which the contribution ceases, never the rate - there is NO gender-differentiated rate, base, floor, ceiling or trigger anywhere in the eight Colombian contributions, and CST art. 143 as amended by Ley 1496 de 2011 expressly forbids salary differences by gender. | Flag | TRUE where the employee is an old-age pensioner in active service; suppresses BOTH the 4% employee and the 12% employer pension contribution | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Trabajador / SubTipoTrabajador (NIE042); PILA T2R2-005, T2R2-006 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R402| CO-FLAG-PROCEDIMIENTO-RETENCION | Withholding procedure election flag | Indicador de Procedimiento de Retencion (1 o 2) | ELECTED BY THE EMPLOYER, PER EMPLOYEE. Once Procedure 2 is elected it applies for the whole six-month period following the June or December calculation, and market practice is to change procedure only at a semester boundary. THE ELECTION MATERIALLY CHANGES JUNE AND DECEMBER NET PAY: under Procedure 1 the statutory minimum prima gets its own table read and is frequently withheld at 0%, while under Procedure 2 it is folded into a single larger base, usually producing higher withholding in that month offset by lower withholding in others. Worked comparison in CCG Module 4.5: on a COP 8,000,000 salary with a COP 4,000,000 first-half prima, Procedure 1 yields COP 92,049.30 for June (the prima read alone falling in the 0% band) against a materially higher single-base result under Procedure 2. | Flag | 1 = ET art. 385 monthly table read, with the statutory minimum prima withheld SEPARATELY; 2 = ET art. 386 fixed percentage set each June and December, binding for the following six months | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Not reported - a configuration flag driving Deducciones / RetencionFuente (NIE177) | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No
R403| CO-FLAG-DEPENDIENTES | Dependants declaration flag and count | Indicador y Numero de Dependientes Economicos | TWO DEDUCTIONS, AND AN EMPLOYEE MAY TAKE BOTH FOR THE SAME DEPENDANT. Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 3: 'el mismo dependiente solo dara lugar a una de estas dos deducciones, EXCEPTO cuando el contribuyente tenga ingresos por rentas de trabajo provenientes de una relacion laboral o legal y reglamentaria, en cuyo caso podran aplicarse ambas deducciones por el mismo dependiente.' The art. 387 deduction is 10% OF GROSS REGARDLESS OF THE NUMBER OF DEPENDANTS - it does not multiply; the art. 336 deduction is per dependant AND SITS OUTSIDE THE 40% AND 1,340-UVT CAPS ('en adicion al limite establecido en el inciso anterior'). WHO QUALIFIES (ET art. 387 par. 2, nums. 2 and 3 as amended by Ley 2411 de 2024 art. 2): children up to 18; children 18-25 whose higher or technical education the taxpayer finances; children over 18 in a certified state of physical or psychological dependence; a spouse or permanent partner dependent by absence of income or with annual income below 260 UVT (COP 13,617,240) certified by a public accountant, or by certified dependence; and parents and siblings on the same conditions. THE DEPENDANT'S IDENTITY DOCUMENT IS REQUIRED, not merely a count - the information return demands the document (numeral N34) and the deduction is not sustainable on audit without it. | Flag | Count of qualifying dependants with each dependant's identity document; drives BOTH the ET art. 387 deduction of 10% of gross capped at 32 UVT/month (COP 1,675,968) AND the art. 336 num. 3 deduction of 72 UVT per dependant per year (COP 3,770,928 each, maximum 4 dependants = 6 UVT = COP 314,244 per dependant per month) | CO-NA | Lump Sum |  | NA | No | No | Regular (System default) | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | Formulario 220 casillas 79-82; Formato 2276 numeral N34 | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). | No


=== SHEET: Employer Sick Pay (dims A1:Z1000, max_row 1000, max_col 26) ===

R1| COLOMBIA - EMPLOYER SICK PAY AND THE INCAPACITY CASCADE (2026)
~
R3| Colombia has NO employer sick-pay scheme of the Czech kind. Cash sickness benefit is a social-security
R4| benefit, and the employer bears only the FIRST TWO DAYS of a general-illness incapacity.
~
R6| WHO PAYS WHAT
R7| General illness, days 1-2 .......... EMPLOYER          66.67% (Mercans default, configurable)
R8| General illness, days 3-90 ......... EPS (health insurer)   66.67% of the contribution base
R9| General illness, days 91-180 ....... EPS                    50.00% of the contribution base
R10| Occupational accident or disease ... ARL (risk insurer)    100.00% of the contribution base,
R11| from the DAY AFTER the event
R12| Maternity leave, 18 weeks .......... EPS   salary earned when the leave begins
R13| Paternity leave, 2 weeks ........... EPS   full salary (expands 1 week per point of structural
R14| unemployment reduction, capped at 5 weeks)
~
R16| FORMULAE
R17| Employer days 1-2 ............. S / 30 x 0.6667 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent
R18| EPS days 3-90 ................. IBC / 30 x 0.6667 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent
R19| EPS days 91-180 ............... IBC / 30 x 0.5000 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent
R20| ARL, occupational ............. IBC / 30 x 1.0000 x d
R21| Maternity ..................... S / 30 x 126 calendar days
R22| Paternity ..................... S / 30 x 14 calendar days
R23| Variable salary ............... average of the year of service preceding the incapacity, or of the
R24| whole period if under a year (CST art. 228)
~
R26| OPEN ITEM 1 - THE RATE FOR THE EMPLOYER'S FIRST TWO DAYS.
R27| Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1, compiled at Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10, allocates 'las
R28| prestaciones economicas correspondientes a los dos (2) primeros dias de incapacidad originada por
R29| enfermedad general' to the EMPLOYER but does NOT state the rate. CST art. 227 sets the auxilio at two
R30| thirds for the first 90 days, and market practice applies 66.67% for days 1-2 on that basis; a
R31| minority practice pays 100%. MERCANS DEFAULT: 66.67%, configurable. Carried in the QA report.
~
R33| OPEN ITEM 2 - THE MINIMUM-WAGE FLOOR ON INCAPACITY PAYMENTS.
R34| Where 66.67% of salary falls below one SMMLV (1,750,905), market practice and settled
R35| administrative doctrine top the payment up to one minimum wage. The primary regulatory source for
R36| that floor was not retrieved this pass. MERCANS DEFAULT: apply the floor, configurable. Carried in
R37| the QA report.
~
R39| PAYROLL TREATMENT (the counter-intuitive part)
R40| Employer days 1-2 ....... NOT salary, but IN the IBC, IN the Ley 21/1982 payroll-levy base and IN
R41| the statutory-benefit base; TAXABLE.
R42| EPS / ARL benefits ...... NOT employer salary, so OUT of the payroll-levy base - but CONTRIBUTIONS
R43| CONTINUE ON THE BASE (PILA change events IGE, IRL, LMA). Exempt within
R44| the statutory minimum (ET art. 206 nums. 1-2 and par. 1, which limits the
R45| exemption to 'las cantidades que correspondan al minimo legal' and taxes
R46| the excess). Advanced by the employer through payroll and recovered from
R47| the insurer; the PILA carries the authorisation number and value
R48| (T2R2-057 to T2R2-060) so the recovery can be offset.
R49| Auxilio de transporte ... NOT PAYABLE for sick days - no travel occurs.
R50| Overtime and premia ..... Not applicable during incapacity.
R51| Benefit accrual ......... Continues (the incapacity payment is in the statutory-benefit base for the
R52| employer's two days); SUSPENDED only for unpaid leave (CST art. 53).
~
R54| Sources: CST arts. 227, 228, 236, 239; Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10;
R55| ET art. 206 nums. 1-2 and par. 1; Ley 361 de 1997 art. 26; Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2;
R56| Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0.

=== SHEET: Tax Credit (dims A1:A101, max_row 101, max_col 1) ===

R1| COLOMBIA - TAX CREDITS: NOT APPLICABLE. THE EQUIVALENT MECHANISMS ARE DEDUCTIONS AND EXEMPT INCOME.
~
R3| Colombia has NO tax credit against the payroll withholding - no personal credit, no child credit, no
R4| disability credit and no non-refundable family credit of the kind the CZ template contemplates. Relief
R5| is delivered instead by SUBTRACTING amounts from the base, in THREE legally distinct classes whose
R6| class determines where each item sits in the ET art. 388 depuration and which caps it consumes.
R7| Getting the class wrong is the most common source of withholding error in Colombia.
~
R9| UVT (tax unit) 2026 = COP 52,374   (Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1; the 2026
R10| uprating was certified at 5.17%, taking the unit from COP 49,799). Every tax parameter is indexed to
R11| the UVT and NO tax threshold is expressed in minimum wages - that is the one-caliber discipline:
R12| minimum wage for social security and labour, UVT for tax. The two are never mixed.
~
R14| CLASS A - NON-TAXABLE INCOME (ingresos no constitutivos de renta ni ganancia ocasional)
R15| Subtracted at step (B), BEFORE the 40% cap reference figure is struck, so it does NOT consume the cap.
R16| A1  Mandatory pension contribution ......... 4% of the IBC .................... ET art. 55
R17| A2  Solidarity pension fund ............... 1% + 0.2-1.0% of the IBC .......... ET art. 55
R18| A3  Mandatory health contribution ......... 4% of the IBC .................... ET art. 56
R19| A4  Voluntary contributions to the individual pension account (RAIS):
R20| the lower of 25% of annual labour income and 2,500 UVT = COP 130,935,000 . ET art. 55
R21| (a non-qualifying withdrawal is withheld at 35%)
R22| A5  Third-party meal payments: not income up to 41 UVT/month = 2,147,334,
R23| where the employee's salary does not exceed 310 UVT = 16,235,940 ..... ET art. 387-1
~
R25| CLASS B - DEDUCTIONS AND EXEMPT INCOME (deducciones y rentas exentas)
R26| Subtracted at steps (D) and (E), INSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps.
R27| B1  Housing-loan interest and monetary correction ... 100 UVT/month = COP 5,237,400 . ET arts. 119, 387
R28| B2  Prepaid medicine / health insurance ............  16 UVT/month = COP   837,984 . ET art. 387 lits. a, b
R29| (the provider must be supervised by the National Health Superintendence or the
R30| Financial Superintendence)
R31| B3  Dependants: 10% of the month's gross labour income, capped 32 UVT = COP 1,675,968 . ET art. 387
R32| NOTE: 10% OF GROSS REGARDLESS OF THE NUMBER OF DEPENDANTS - it does not multiply.
R33| B4  Voluntary pension funds + AFC / AVC long-term savings: the lower of 30% of labour
R34| income and 3,800 UVT/year = COP 199,021,200, SHARED between the two vehicles . ET arts. 126-1, 126-4
R35| B5  THE 25% EXEMPT-INCOME DEDUCTION ................ 790 UVT/year = COP 41,375,460 . ET art. 206 num. 10
R36| COMPUTED LAST, on the figure net of non-taxable income, the art. 387 deductions and all other
R37| exempt income: 'El calculo de esta renta exenta se efectuara una vez se detraiga del valor
R38| total de los pagos laborales recibidos por el trabajador, los ingresos no constitutivos de
R39| renta, las deducciones y las demas rentas exentas diferentes a la establecida en el presente
R40| numeral.' Computing 25% of GROSS overstates it and under-withholds.
R41| Monthly apportionment used: 65.8333 UVT = 3,447,955.
R42| B6  Cesantias and their interest: exempt in full where the average monthly income of the last six
R43| months is <= 350 UVT = 18,330,900; then tapering 90 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0%
R44| through 410 / 470 / 530 / 590 / 650 UVT ......................... ET art. 206 num. 4
R45| They are EXCLUDED from the monthly base entirely under both procedures, and the 25%
R46| exemption does NOT apply to them (art. 206 par. 2).
R47| B7  Pension income: taxable only above 1,000 UVT/month = COP 52,374,000 ...... ET art. 206 num. 5
R48| B8  Accident, illness and maternity-protection indemnities and burial expenses: exempt ONLY up to
R49| the statutory minimum amount ................................. ET art. 206 nums. 1-3, par. 1
~
R51| CLASS C - OUTSIDE THE CAPS ENTIRELY
R52| C1  Additional dependants deduction: 72 UVT per dependant per year = COP 3,770,928 each,
R53| maximum 4 dependants = COP 15,083,712/year, i.e. 6 UVT = COP 314,244 per dependant
R54| per month ..................................... ET art. 336 num. 3, second paragraph
R55| 'en adicion al limite establecido en el inciso anterior' - it is applied AFTER and OUTSIDE
R56| the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps.
R57| STACKING: an EMPLOYEE may take BOTH B3 and C1 FOR THE SAME DEPENDANT. Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 3:
R58| 'el mismo dependiente solo dara lugar a una de estas dos deducciones, excepto cuando el
R59| contribuyente tenga ingresos por rentas de trabajo provenientes de una relacion laboral o
R60| legal y reglamentaria, en cuyo caso podran aplicarse ambas deducciones por el mismo
R61| dependiente.'
~
R63| THE COMBINED CAP
R64| (D + E) <= 40% of (C = A - B)   AND   (D + E) <= 1,340 UVT/year = COP 70,181,160
R65| Monthly apportionment used: 111.6667 UVT = 5,848,430.
R66| OPEN ITEM: the 790-UVT and 1,340-UVT caps are stated ANNUALLY yet withholding is monthly. Market
R67| practice, which Mercans follows, apportions them at one twelfth and tests each month against the
R68| apportioned figure. No regulation prescribes the apportionment expressly. Carried in the QA report.
R69| The CURRENT year's UVT applies to the annual caps (DIAN Concepto 015007 int. 973 de 13 septiembre 2023).
~
R71| NOT AVAILABLE AGAINST WITHHOLDING
R72| The 1% / 240-UVT invoice-based deduction of ET art. 336 num. 5 is expressly excluded - 'no se tendra
R73| en cuenta para el calculo de la retencion en la fuente' - confirmed by DIAN Concepto 015007 int. 973
R74| de 13 septiembre 2023. It is available only in the employee's own annual return.
~
R76| NOT A CREDIT, AND NOT A TAX
R77| The Contribucion Solidaria a la Educacion Superior (Contribucion Sabes) is a CST art. 150 permitted
R78| DEDUCTION added by Ley 1911 de 2018 art. 22, not a tax and not a credit - it must never be
R79| aggregated with the retencion en la fuente on Formulario 350.
~
R81| DECLARATIONS THE EMPLOYEE MUST MAKE (no relief is automatic except the 25%)
R82| Housing-loan interest ... lender certificate.  Prepaid health ... provider certificate.
R83| Dependants .............. number, EACH DEPENDANT'S IDENTITY DOCUMENT, relationship and basis of
R84| dependency; for a dependent spouse, parent or sibling, a public
R85| accountant's certificate of annual income below 260 UVT (COP 13,617,240)
R86| or a certified state of physical or psychological dependence.
R87| Voluntary pension / AFC / AVC / RAIS ... fund or administrator certificates.
R88| Higher withholding rate ... written request, effective the month AFTER it is made (ET art. 383 par. 3).
R89| WHO QUALIFIES AS A DEPENDANT (ET art. 387 par. 2 nums. 2-3 as amended by Ley 2411 de 2024 art. 2):
R90| children up to 18; children 18-25 whose higher or technical education the taxpayer finances;
R91| children over 18 in a certified state of dependence; a spouse or permanent partner dependent by
R92| absence of income or with annual income below 260 UVT; parents and siblings on the same conditions.
~
R94| OUT OF MERCANS SCOPE BUT FLAGGED SO THE ENGINE DOES NOT SILENTLY CLAMP THEM
R95| ET art. 206 par. 4 and Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 place art. 206 nums. 6-9, the
R96| prima especial and the prima de costo de vida OUTSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT limits. These attach to
R97| military, judicial, diplomatic and public university staff - out of private-sector scope.
~
R99| NON-RESIDENTS GET NONE OF THE ABOVE
R100| ET art. 408: a flat 20% on the GROSS payment, with no depuration, no art. 387 deductions, no 25%
R101| exempt income and no tax-free band. DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026.


=== SHEET: Global WTC Coverage (dims A1:C31, max_row 31, max_col 3) ===

R1| # | Item | Detail
R2| 1 | Global baseline file | shared/Global_WTC_V1_0.xlsx
R3| 2 | Global codes in the baseline | 300
R4| 3 | Global codes included in the Colombia WTC | 300 - ALL of them. None excluded. Every code configured FRESH for Colombia 2026 against CO-CCG-001 Modules 3-7 and the primary law in 00-official-sources/pdfs/.
R5| 4 | Global codes excluded | 0
R6| 5 | Colombia-specific additions | 102 CO-prefixed wage types: 55 earnings (core pay, the six premium hour types, the statutory benefits, the leave cascade, the termination heads), 19 employee deductions, 11 employer contributions and accruals, 17 bases / intermediaries / rates / flags.
R7| 6 | Total rows in the Wage Type Catalogue | 402
R8| 7 | SS-fund column expansion (why five, not one) | Colombia has EIGHT mandatory contributions across THREE insurance branches, THREE payroll levies and ONE employee-only solidarity surcharge, and they do NOT share a base. Q1 = Salud (EE 4% + ER 8.5%, IBC, cap 25 SMMLV, ER exonerable). Q2 = Fondo de Solidaridad Pensional (EE only, 1% from 4 SMMLV plus 0.2-1.0% from 16 SMMLV, IBC). Q3 = Riesgos Laborales / ARL (ER only, 0.348-8.7% by work centre, IBC). Q4 = Parafiscales CCF 4% + SENA 2% + ICBF 3% on the LEY 21 DE 1982 ART. 17 BASE WITH NO CEILING. R = Pension (EE 4% + ER 12%, IBC, cap 25 SMMLV, never exonerable). Collapsing Q4 into the others would silently cap the levies at 25 SMMLV and would drop paid rest days from their base.
R9| 8 | Three Colombia-specific base columns added (S, T, U) | S = the HARD CST art. 127 / 128 salary flag, which the nomina electronica demands at ELEMENT LEVEL through paired S / NS element names and which must never be derived at reporting time. T = the statutory-benefit base (prima / cesantias / intereses), which INCLUDES the auxilio de transporte even though the IBC and the levy base exclude it. U = the vacation base of CST art. 192, which excludes ONLY rest-day work and supplementary overtime hours, so the night premium and commissions stay in. Values: Salary / Non-Salary / Paid Rest / Conditional / NA for S; Yes / No / Yes-No / NA for T and U.
R10| 9 | Column meaning adopted (N, O, P) | N = subject to retencion en la fuente at all. O = subject to the ET art. 383 progressive table (0 / 19 / 28 / 33 / 35 / 37 / 39% on the art. 388 depurated base). P = SPECIAL TAX is used for the NON-RESIDENT flat 20% on gross of ET art. 408 - the treatment that produces a 17-fold withholding difference on the same salary and is the single largest configuration risk in Colombian expatriate payroll.
R11| 10 | Yes-No entries and their justification | Every Yes-No (rendered 'Yes/No') marks a genuine statutory conditionality, and column D carries the explanation in every case, as the v2.5 C3 guard requires. The recurring reasons are: (a) the LEY 1393/2010 ART. 30 40% CLAMP, which excludes a validly pacted non-salary payment from the IBC only up to 40% of total remuneration and forces the excess back in; (b) the ET art. 387-1 meal thresholds; (c) the ET art. 206 num. 4 cesantias taper; (d) the ET art. 206 par. 1 statutory-minimum limit on EPS / ARL benefit exemptions; (e) the apprentice's phase-dependent regime; (f) the CST art. 130 viaticos split; and (g) the conditional character of an anticipo.
R12| 11 | 2026 parameters used, and their verification | SMMLV 1,750,905 (Decreto 1469 de 2025 art. 1 - VERIFIED in the source PDF, which also records that the Council of State suspended the decree on 12 February 2026, that Decreto 0159 de 2026 re-fixed the identical amount, and that the suspension was revoked on 9 July 2026, so the AMOUNT was never in dispute). UVT 52,374 (Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15-12-2025 art. 1 - VERIFIED in the source PDF). Auxilio de transporte 249,095 (Decreto 1470 de 2025 art. 1 - the decree itself is not in 00-official-sources; the figure is carried from CO-CCG-001 and is corroborated by the exact COP 2,000,000 combined minimum cash entitlement). IBC ceiling 25 SMMLV = 43,772,625 and salario integral at 70% (Ley 797 de 2003 art. 5 - VERIFIED verbatim). 40% clamp (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 - VERIFIED verbatim). Levy base including paid rest days (Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 - VERIFIED verbatim). Rest-day premium 80% -> 90% on 1 July 2026 (Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 14 - VERIFIED verbatim). Night window 19:00-06:00 (Ley 2466 art. 10 - VERIFIED verbatim). 42-hour week (Ley 2466 art. 11 - VERIFIED verbatim). Salary in kind 50% / 30% (CST art. 129 - VERIFIED verbatim). Vacation base (CST art. 192 - VERIFIED verbatim). Viaticos (CST art. 130 - VERIFIED verbatim). Tips (CST art. 131 - VERIFIED verbatim). Dotacion every four months (CST arts. 230, 232 - VERIFIED verbatim). Apprentice 75% / 100% (Ley 2466 art. 21 - VERIFIED verbatim). Connectivity allowance (Ley 2466 art. 53 - VERIFIED verbatim). Cesantias 8.33% monthly advance (Ley 2466 art. 64 - VERIFIED verbatim).
R13| 12 | Newest-law check (control C2) | LEY 2381 DE 2024, the complete replacement of the Colombian pension architecture, is the newest pension law on the statute book and it does NOT govern any 2026 payroll period. Its own art. 94 set entry into force at 1 July 2025; Constitutional Court Auto 841 de 18 junio 2025 SUSPENDED that entry into force; and Judgment C-264 de 25 agosto 2026 upheld art. 94 on the understanding that the upheld provisions take effect on 1 APRIL 2027. Every 2026 period therefore runs on Ley 100 de 1993 as amended by Ley 797 de 2003, and NO part of Ley 2381 is configured as live. What changes in 2027: only the solidarity-fund surcharge, which becomes a single five-band 1.5% / 1.8% / 2.5% / 2.8% / 3.0% scale - the 16% total, the 12 / 4 split and the 25-SMMLV ceiling are unchanged. Recorded as a 2027 change-control item.
R14| 13 | Configuration source | Configured fresh for Colombia from CO-CCG-001 v1.0 Modules 3-10, the 04-reports-spec files, and the primary law in 00-official-sources/pdfs/ (CST Decreto 2663 de 1950, Ley 100 de 1993, Ley 797 de 2003, Ley 1122 de 2007, Ley 1393 de 2010, Ley 21 de 1982, Ley 89 de 1988, Ley 2101 de 2021, Ley 2381 de 2024, Ley 2466 de 2025, Decreto 1469 de 2025, Decreto 0223 de 2026, Decreto 1990 de 2016, Decreto Ley 624 de 1989, Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003, Resoluciones DIAN 000227 y 000238 de 2025, Resolucion 2388 de 2016 y Anexo 2, Circular MinTrabajo 0101 de 2025, Oficio DIAN 13840 de 2025). NOT copied from Czechia, Cote d'Ivoire or any other country.
R15| 14 | DERIVED flags | (1) The Mercans code numbers for the 102 Colombia-specific rows are DERIVED - no Mercans numeric code range has been assigned for Colombia, so mnemonic CO- codes are used and must be renumbered at system configuration. (2) The auxilio de transporte amount is carried from the CCG because Decreto 1470 de 2025 is not in 00-official-sources. (3) The additional high-risk pension rate is read from the Resolucion 2388 rate table (26% total against 16% standard, implying +10 pp employer-borne), NOT from the implementing decree - see the open items below.
R16| 15 | Open items carried to the QA report | (1) The rate for the employer's first two sick days - Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10 allocates the days but not the rate; Mercans default 66.67%, configurable. (2) The minimum-wage floor on incapacity payments - primary source not retrieved; Mercans default applies the floor, configurable. (3) The additional employer pension contribution for Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003 high-risk activities - the 2026 rate and base not confirmed from a primary regulatory source. (4) The monthly apportionment of the annual 790-UVT and 1,340-UVT tax caps - no regulation prescribes it expressly; Mercans applies one twelfth.
R17| 16 | Egypt F20 guards | COMMISSION IS PRESENT as a first-class wage type (CO-COMISIONES) with its own nomina electronica element, and the global commission-bearing codes are configured, not dropped. EVERY tax code in column G is drawn from the Colombian legend below (CO-TX-01 to CO-TX-10 and CO-NA) and the validator rejects any code that does not start with 'CO-'. NO Yes-No appears in a taxability or subjection column without an explanation in column D.
~
R19| TAX CODE LEGEND (C9 - every code defined and sourced to a Colombian authority publication)
R20| Tax Code | Meaning | Official Source
R21| CO-TX-01 | Ordinary taxable work income - RESIDENT. Enters the total taxable payments of the month, is depurated under ET art. 388 (non-taxable income, art. 387 deductions, exempt income, the 40% cap and the 1,340-UVT annual cap) and is withheld on the ET art. 383 progressive table (0/19/28/33/35/37/39%) under Procedure 1 (art. 385) or at the fixed percentage under Procedure 2 (art. 386). No preferential rate and no tax-free allowance exists for any individual earning type. | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374).
R22| CO-TX-02 | Severance savings and the interest on them (cesantias e intereses). EXCLUDED from the monthly withholding base under BOTH procedures - they never enter the monthly table read. Exempt IN FULL where the average monthly income of the last 6 months is <= 350 UVT (18,330,900); above that the untaxed portion tapers 90/80/60/40/20/0% through 650 UVT. The 25% exemption of art. 206 num. 10 does NOT apply to them (art. 206 par. 2). | ET arts. 206 num. 4 and par. 2, 385, 386; Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99; Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 (8.33% monthly advance deposit).
R23| CO-TX-03 | Statutory minimum service bonus (prima de servicios legal). Under Procedure 1 it is withheld SEPARATELY, on its own read of the ET art. 383 table, and is NOT added to the month's salary. Under Procedure 2 it is folded into the ordinary monthly base (the 13 divisor of art. 386 already prorates its effect). Any excess above the statutory minimum bonus goes to the ordinary monthly base. | ET arts. 385 and 386; CST arts. 306 (as amended by Ley 1788 de 2016 art. 1), 307 and 308.
R24| CO-TX-04 | Non-taxable income - ingreso no constitutivo de renta ni ganancia ocasional. Subtracted at step (B) of the ET art. 388 depuration, i.e. BEFORE the 40% cap reference figure is struck, which makes it more valuable than exempt income. Covers the employee mandatory pension 4%, the solidarity pension fund 1% + 0.2-1.0%, the mandatory health 4%, and voluntary contributions to the individual pension account within 25% of annual labour income / 2,500 UVT. | ET arts. 55 and 56; ET art. 388 num. 1; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N23 and N24 (which group the pension and solidarity-fund contributions on a single line).
R25| CO-TX-05 | Conditionally not income - third-party meal payments (pagos de alimentacion a terceros). NOT income to the employee where BOTH conditions hold: the employee's salary does not exceed 310 UVT (16,235,940) AND the payment is made to a THIRD PARTY. Where the month's payments exceed 41 UVT (2,147,334) the EXCESS is taxable employment income. Cash paid to the employee for food is salary and gets none of this treatment. | ET art. 387-1; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N08 (excess) and N32 (within-limit amount), which report the two portions separately; Formulario 220 casillas 37 and 38.
R26| CO-TX-06 | Not income and not remuneration - amounts given not to enrich the employee but to enable the work: statutory work clothing (dotacion), work tools, protective equipment, means of transport, representation expenses, occasional viaticos for transport, and genuine documented business-expense reimbursement. Outside EVERY base - tax, contribution, payroll-levy, statutory-benefit and vacation. | CST arts. 128 and 230 (art. 230 as modified by Ley 11 de 1984 art. 7); CST arts. 130 and 232; ET art. 387-1 final paragraph (representation expenses expressly outside the meal rule).
R27| CO-TX-07 | Exempt income LIMITED TO THE STATUTORY MINIMUM AMOUNT - accident and illness indemnities, maternity-protection indemnities and employee burial expenses, and by extension the sickness, maternity, paternity and occupational-injury cash benefits paid by the EPS or the ARL. ET art. 206 par. 1 is explicit that the exemption 'operates only on the amounts corresponding to the statutory minimum' and that the excess is not exempt. | ET art. 206 nums. 1, 2, 3 and par. 1; CST arts. 227, 236; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10 (Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1, employer days 1-2).
R28| CO-TX-08 | NON-RESIDENT flat withholding - 20% ON THE GROSS PAYMENT, with NO art. 388 depuration, NO 25% exempt income, NO art. 387 deductions and NO tax-free band, for Colombian-source work income. Determined at the moment of EACH payment on the status then held; the switch to the art. 383 table is PROSPECTIVE from the payment following the 183rd day, never retroactive. Where the service is performed entirely outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and there is NO withholding. | ET arts. 10, 408-415; DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026 (upholding Concepto 1789 int. 14595 de 2025); DIAN Oficio 28380 de 2019; Formulario 350 casillas 109-112 and hoja 2 casillas 141-155.
R29| CO-TX-09 | Exempt income - renta exenta - for voluntary pension fund contributions (art. 126-1) and construction-savings / long-term-savings account contributions (arts. 126-4 AFC/AVC), employee or employer-paid on the employee's behalf. Subtracted at step (E) INSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps, within a single shared annual limit of the lower of 30% of labour income or 3,800 UVT (COP 199,021,200 for 2026). | ET arts. 126-1 and 126-4; ET art. 388 num. 2; Formulario 220 casillas 56, 57 and 58; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N26, N27 and N28.
R30| CO-TX-10 | Pension income and refunds of pension savings. A retirement, invalidity, old-age, survivor or occupational-risk pension is taxable only on the monthly amount exceeding 1,000 UVT (COP 52,374,000). The 40% limit of art. 388 expressly does not apply to pension payments, pension substitution indemnities or refunds of pension savings. Paid by the pension administrator, so OUT OF SCOPE of employer gross-to-net per scope_manifest; retained for completeness and for Formulario 220 casilla 50 / Formato 2276 numeral N21. | ET arts. 206 num. 5 and par. 3, 383 (first paragraph, which brings pensions into the same table), 388 final sentence; Formulario 220 casilla 50; Formato 2276 v4 numeral N21.
R31| CO-NA | Not a remuneration line - an employee deduction, an employer contribution or accrual, a calculation base, an intermediary, a notional, a rate or a configuration flag. Every taxability and subjection column is NA because the row is a mechanism, not a payment to the employee. | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000013 de 2021 Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2).
